Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on nucleus structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 943. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 367 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the nucleus space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 943 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 367. QED.
